--- Commit Message ---
LU-19783 lfsck: fix MDT orphan by LFSCK

This patch adds a new option "-O | --mdt-orphan" in "lfsck_start"
to check the possible MDT orphan, which is no "linkEA", owned by
non-root user and group, old enough since its creation time,
(the timeout can be specified by "-T | --mdt-orphan-timeout"), and
the size is 0. It will be moved into ".lustre/lost+found".

Before recording a possible orphan MDT-object during the first-stage
scanning, check and skip it if it is still tracked in the MDT PENDING
directory, because these objects will be handled by the MDD orphan
subsystem.

The "struct lfsck_bookmark" is modified, then this patch adds the
corresponding check during setup and load.

It introduces a new failure injection "OBD_FAIL_LFSCK_MDT_ORPHAN"
to create an orphan object in MDT, which will be fixed by LFSCK
in the newly added test in sanity-lfsck.

Use plain bit numbers in "enum lu_object_header_flags" instead of
BIT(n), since loh_flags is only used with set_bit()/test_bit(),
which take a bit number rather than a mask, BIT(n) only worked by
accident, and the next flags would eventually overflow loh_flags.

It also adds check on "test_45" in "sanity-lfsck" to verify LFSCK
is completed, which should check on "SINGLEMDS" instead of "ost1",
otherwise the LFSCK could still be running on the "SINGLEMDS" and
affect the follwing test.

Test-Parameters: testlist=sanity-lfsck serverversion=2.16.1
Signed-off-by: Hongchao Zhang <hongchao@whamcloud.com>

Here's summary of my changes:
Excellent! The variable `val` is properly declared at line 277 with all other local variables at the beginning of the function. This conforms to C89 variable declaration rules. The fix is complete.

## Summary of the Fix

**Issue:** The patch introduced a compile error on line 404 of `lustre/utils/lustre_lfsck.c` where an undeclared variable `progname` was used in an error message.

**Rationale:** 
1. The `progname` variable was used in the new error message in the `-T` option handling code (line 404), but this variable was never declared or defined in `lustre_lfsck.c`.
2. While `progname` is a common pattern in some Lustre utilities (like `lfs.c`), the `lustre_lfsck.c` file doesn't use this pattern anywhere else.
3. Other error messages in the same file (e.g., lines 298-303, 311-316, 324-329, 337-340, etc.) simply use `fprintf(stderr, ...)` without any program name prefix.

**Fix:** Removed the `progname,` parameter from the `fprintf` call on lines 403-404 to match the error message formatting style used consistently throughout the rest of the file.

The fixed error message now reads:
```c
fprintf(stderr, "invalid timeout '%s' for MDT orphan, should be >= 1\n", optarg);
```

This is consistent with the error handling pattern used elsewhere in the file and eliminates the undefined variable reference.
